# Point-in-time Restore Specification - revision 4.3 (working draft)

WORKING DRAFT - NOT APPROVED. This draft is circulated for review and is not in force. Appendix J (job rename crosswalk) was approved on its own under a separate change record; see the document control register.

## 5. Backup set chain (proposed)

5.2 The snapshot time of a set is the job end time reported by the job history; server logs are no longer consulted. 5.5 FAILED and CANCELLED sets break the chain. A set whose snapshot equals the restore point is after it.

## 6. Catalog replay (proposed)

6.2 The display spelling of a path follows the latest entry applied to it. 6.3 Void entries are ignored when they sit in a different part than the entry they void.

## 7. Exclusion patterns (proposed)

'*' matches any run of characters including '/'. Patterns are case-sensitive. The newest catalogue revision applies from its publication.

## 10. Allocation (proposed)

Sizes round to the nearest cluster; hard links and resident files allocate like any other file.
